d. 其他未量化的影响
d. Other Unquantified Impacts
(1) 预算编制及金融交易管理成本
(1) Costs for Budgeting and Administering Financial Transactions
鉴于拟议的OPT费用金额显著高于现行学生及交流访问者项目(SEVP)费用,国土安全部预计经SEVP认证的机构及保管其资金的金融机构都将加强审查。推荐F-1非移民学生参加OPT的经SEVP认证机构,需要考虑缴纳拟议费用的成本,包括预算规划及资金来源的确定。虽然拟议规则并未限制学校为OPT费用提供资金的方式,但国土安全部预计受监管实体将建立预算编制流程,以评估其成本、财务能力及相关会计与审计流程。这可能导致金融交易方面产生额外的行政负担。
Given that the proposed OPT fee amounts are significantly higher than the current SEVP fees, DHS anticipates increased scrutiny from both SEVP-certified institutions and the financial institutions that retain their funds. SEVP-certified institutions recommending F-1 nonimmigrant students for OPT would need to consider the costs for paying the proposed fees, including budget planning and identifying funding sources. While the proposed rule does not restrict how schools may fund OPT fees, DHS expects that regulated entities would establish budgeting processes to assess their costs, financial capacity, and related accounting and audit processes. This may result in additional administrative burdens with financial transactions.
尽管经SEVP认证的机构已经向国土安全部提交费用缴纳,但拟议的变更可能改变其流程。机构必须针对每位OPT参与者的每次OPT事件分别提交付款,但机构可以选择按周期(例如在学年末或学年初)提交此类付款。[134]这可能需要加强预算处理流程,以确保每次OPT推荐都有充足资金。学校可能需要为这些交易制定记录保存及跟踪机制,以支持审计并保留合规证据。
Although SEVP-certified institutions already submit fee payments to DHS, the proposed changes may alter their processes. Institutions must submit separate payments per OPT occurrence for each OPT participant, however, institutions may choose to submit these payments on a periodic basis ( e.g., at the end or beginning of the academic year).[134] This could require enhanced budget processing to ensure sufficient funds are available for each OPT recommendation. Schools may need to develop recordkeeping and tracking mechanisms for these transactions to support auditing and maintain evidence of compliance.
金融实体(例如银行)可能因处理此类交易、审核机构可用资金及核实付款授权所需的时间增加而产生额外成本。在机构从学生、雇主或其他实体获得资金的情形下,受监管实体和外部各方可能需要花费更多时间来建立付款方式,并对这些资金进行跟踪和管理。
Financial entities ( e.g., banks) may incur additional costs due to increased time spent processing these transactions, reviewing institutions' available funds, and validating payment authorizations. In cases where institutions receive funding from students, employers or other entities, there may be further time burdens to establish payment methods and to track and administer these funds for both the regulated entities and external parties.
国土安全部还需要建立接收和确认费用缴纳的流程。这些活动可能需要额外的时间以及潜在的信息技术系统更新,以便按经SEVP认证机构及OPT申请检索和存储相关数据。其他机构,例如美国财政部,可能也需要承担成本以修改其系统来适应新费用。
DHS would also need to establish processes to receive and confirm fee payments. These activities may require additional time and potential IT system updates to retrieve and store relevant data by SEVP-certified institution and OPT request. Other agencies, such as the Treasury of the United States, may also incur costs to modify their systems to accommodate the new fees.
由于与这些金融交易相关的各种情形和条件各不相同,且本规则引入了一项新费用,国土安全部无法估算这些流程的成本影响。国土安全部请求就这些潜在负担提交意见。
Due to the various scenarios and conditions associated with these financial transactions, and because this rule introduces a new fee, DHS is unable to estimate the cost impacts of these processes. DHS requests comments on these potential burdens.
(2) 退款成本
(2) Costs for Refunds
国土安全部预计,在某些情况下,教育机构可能会申请退还OPT费用。拟议规则允许在经移民及海关执法局(ICE)审查后予以退款。教育机构将承担提交退款申请所需的时间负担,而ICE将承担审查和处理这些申请的成本。如果退款获批,ICE还将承担将资金退还给教育机构并提供交易确认的负担。国土安全部目前没有足够的信息来估算退款申请的数量或相关成本。国土安全部请求就这些潜在负担和影响提交意见。
DHS anticipates that educational institutions may, in some cases, request refunds of the OPT fee. The proposed rule allows for refunds subject to ICE review. Educational institutions would incur time burdens to submit refund requests, and ICE would incur costs to review and process these requests. If refunds are granted, ICE would also bear the burden of transferring funds back to the educational institution and providing confirmation of these transactions. DHS does not have sufficient information to estimate the number of refund applications or the associated costs. DHS requests comments on these potential burdens and impacts.
(3) 对完成前OPT参与的影响
(3) Impact on Participation in Pre-Completion OPT
初始费用7万美元适用于F-1非移民首次参加OPT的情形,无论是完成学业前OPT还是完成学业后OPT。后续参加OPT需缴纳3万美元费用,无论是完成学业前OPT、完成学业后OPT,还是STEM OPT延期。除STEM延期外,OPT参加时间总计限制为12个月;完成学业前OPT将减少可用于完成学业后OPT的时间。因此,若F-1非移民同时参加完成学业前OPT和完成学业后OPT,将被征收总计10万美元的费用,而若整个OPT期间均发生在完成学业后,则为7万美元。美国国土安全部(DHS)预计,各院校将鼓励F-1非移民仅使用完成学业后OPT,以便在相同的OPT授权总期限内利用费用差异获益。尽管DHS预计未来参加完成学业前OPT的情况将减少,但DHS无法合理预测这一减少的程度(印刷页第64591页)。DHS请求就此讨论提出意见并提供任何可用数据,以进一步完善这些估算。
The initial fee of $70,000 applies when an F-1 nonimmigrant participates in OPT for the first time, whether it is pre-completion or post-completion OPT. A subsequent OPT participation incurs a $30,000 fee, regardless of whether it is pre-completion OPT, post-completion OPT, or a STEM OPT extension. OPT participation, excluding the STEM extension, is limited to a total of 12 months; pre-completion OPT reduces the amount of time available for post-completion OPT. Consequently, a total fee of $100,000 would be assessed should the F-1 nonimmigrant engage in pre-completion and post-completion OPT, compared to $70,000 if the entire OPT period occurs post-completion. DHS anticipates that institutions would encourage F-1 nonimmigrants to use only post-completion OPT to benefit from the fee differential for the same total authorized OPT period. While DHS expects future participation in pre-completion OPT to decline, DHS cannot reasonably predict the extent of this ( printed page 64591) decrease. DHS requests comments and any available data regarding this discussion which could further improve these estimates.
6. 益处
6. Benefits
正如本拟议规则第四节所述,DHS已发现F-1非移民利用OPT进行欺诈,以滞留超期或在其学习项目之外非法工作。DHS认为本拟议规则将降低OPT项目中的欺诈和滥用风险。与目前教育机构在无金钱风险的情况下为F-1学生推荐OPT的流程相比,DHS认为拟议规则可能会导致指定校方官员(DSO)在使用OPT推荐方面受到更多审查。
As discussed in section IV of this proposed rule, DHS has identified fraudulent use of OPT by F-1 nonimmigrants to overstay or illegally work outside of their program of study. DHS believes this proposed rule would reduce fraud and abuse risks in the OPT program. Compared to the current process, in which educational institutions recommend F-1 students for OPT without monetary risk, DHS believes that the proposed rule would likely result in more scrutiny of the use of OPT recommendation by DSOs.
拟议的监管财务义务将促使学校确保OPT机会具有真正的教育价值,这与实习培训项目的初衷相符。该规则将激励学校仅为最有前途的学生推荐OPT,从而减少“签证工厂”活动,并改善对移民法规的遵守。该规则还可能对那些原本计划利用这些非移民身份从事欺诈或滥用行为的人起到威慑作用。
The proposed regulatory financial obligation would encourage schools to ensure OPT opportunities provide genuine educational value, aligning with the original intent of the practical training program. The rule would incentivize schools to recommend only the most promising students for OPT, reducing “visa mill” activities and improving compliance with immigration regulations. The rule could also serve as a deterrent to those who would otherwise plan to engage in fraud or abuse of these nonimmigrant classifications.
在OPT参与人数减少的范围内,可能会出现向其他可能履行与OPT中F-1非移民学生相同工作职能的工人的转移。该规则可能为美国工人提供就业机会,而在没有该规则的情况下,这些机会本会被OPT学生占据。DHS意图通过本规则减少劳动力市场中低薪外国工人的过剩,从而增加美国工人的就业机会,并尽量减少可能的工资压低现象。
To the extent that OPT participation decreases, there could be transfers to other workers who may perform the same job functions as the F-1 nonimmigrant students in OPT. The rule may provide job opportunities to U.S. workers who would have lost job opportunities to OPT students in the absence of the rule. DHS intends for the rule to reduce an excess of low-paid foreign workers in the labor market, thereby increasing job opportunities for U.S. workers and minimizing possible wage depression.
此外,根据现行法律,许多参加OPT的F-1非移民学生及其雇主根据《联邦保险纳税法》(FICA)无需缴纳社会保障和医疗保险工资税。这一豁免可能激励雇主聘用参加OPT的F-1非移民学生,而非需缴纳FICA工资税的美国公民或其他人员。参加OPT并获得工作授权的F-1非移民学生数量减少,可能为美国工人提供就业转移机会。在部分参加OPT的F-1非移民学生被需缴纳FICA税的工人取代的范围内,联邦税收征收方面可能会出现额外的转移。
Further, under current law, many F-1 nonimmigrant students in OPT and their employers are exempt from paying the Social Security and Medicare payroll taxes under the Federal Insurance Contributions Act (FICA). This exemption may provide an incentive to hire F-1 nonimmigrant students in OPT over U.S. citizens or others subject to FICA payroll taxes. The availability of fewer F-1 nonimmigrant students with employment authorization through OPT could provide job transfers to U.S. workers. To the extent that some F-1 nonimmigrant students in OPT are replaced with FICA-subject workers, there could be additional transfers for federal taxes collected.
B. 《监管灵活性法案》
B. Regulatory Flexibility Act
1980年《监管灵活性法案》(RFA),经修订的《美国法典》第5编第601-612节,要求联邦机构考虑拟议规则是否会对大量小实体产生重大影响。“小实体”一词包括独立拥有和经营、在其领域内不占主导地位的小企业、非营利组织,以及人口少于50,000人的政府辖区。DHS请求公众提供信息和数据,以帮助更好地理解本拟议规则对小实体的影响。DHS还征求公众对能够实现相同目标并最大限度减少拟议规则对小实体经济影响的替代方案的意见。
The Regulatory Flexibility Act of 1980 (RFA), 5 U.S.C. 601-612, as amended, requires Federal agencies to consider whether the proposed rule would have a significant impact on a substantial number of small entities. The term “small entities” is comprised of small business, not-for-profit organizations that are independently owned and operated and are not dominant in their fields and governmental jurisdictions with populations of less than 50,000. DHS requests information and data from the public that would assist in better understanding the impact of this proposed rule on small entities. DHS also seeks input from the public on alternatives that would accomplish the same objectives and minimize the proposed rule's economic impact on small entities.
《监管灵活性法案》第603(b)节规定了初步监管灵活性分析的内容,涉及以下方面:
Section 603(b) of the Regulatory Flexibility Act prescribes the content of the Initial Regulatory Flexibility Analysis, which addresses the following:
1. 说明该机构考虑采取行动的原因;
1. A description of the reasons why action by the agency is being considered;
2. 简要说明本拟议规则的目标及法律依据;
2. A succinct statement of the objectives of, and legal basis for, this proposed rule;
3. 本拟议规则适用的小实体数量的描述,以及在可行的情况下,对相关小实体数量的估计;或说明为何无法提供此类估计;
3. A description of and, where feasible, an estimate of the number of small entities to which this proposed rule will apply, or an Explanation of Why No Such Estimate is Available;
4. 本拟议规则预计所带来的报告、记录保存及其他合规要求的描述,包括对将受该要求约束的各类小实体的估计,以及编制报告或记录所需的专业技能类型;
4. A description of the projected reporting, recordkeeping, and other compliance requirements of this proposed rule, including an estimate of the classes of small entities that will be subject to the requirement and the type of professional skills necessary for preparation of the report or record;
5. 在可行范围内,对可能与本拟议规则重复、重叠或冲突的所有相关联邦规则的识别;以及
5. An identification, to the extent practicable, of all relevant Federal rules that may duplicate, overlap, or conflict with this proposed rule; and
6. 对任何能够实现适用法规所陈述目标,并最大限度减少本拟议规则对小实体造成的重大经济影响的重大替代方案的描述。
6. A description of any significant alternatives to this proposed rule that accomplish the stated objectives of applicable statutes and minimize any significant economic impact of the proposed rule on small entities.
1. 机构考虑采取该行动的原因说明
1. A Description of the Reasons Why the Action by the Agency Is Being Considered
美国国土安全部(DHS)提议对选择性实习培训(OPT)收取新费用,以打击欺诈行为,加强移民制度的完整性,保护美国工人,并减轻与OPT调查相关的国土安全部资源负担。
DHS proposes new fees for OPT to combat fraud, strengthen the integrity of the immigration system, protect U.S. workers, and alleviate the burdens imposed on DHS resources tied to OPT investigations.
2. 对拟议规则目标及法律依据的简要说明
2. A Succinct Statement of the Objectives of, and Legal Basis for, the Proposed Rule
拟议规则将规定,当学生与交流访问者项目认证(SEVP认证)机构首次推荐F-1非移民学生从事OPT时,收取70,000美元的费用,此后该学生每次获得后续OPT推荐时,收取30,000美元的费用。该费用有助于通过鼓励机构对OPT文件进行更深入的审查来打击欺诈行为。
The proposed rule would establish a $70,000 fee applicable to SEVP-certified institutions when they initially recommend an F-1 nonimmigrant student to engage in OPT and a $30,000 fee each time there is a subsequent OPT recommendation for that student. The fee helps to combat fraud by encouraging institutions to conduct a more in-depth review of OPT documentation.
根据2002年《国土安全法》(HSA)(公法107-296号,116 Stat. 2135)第102条、6 U.S.C. 112条,以及《移民与国籍法》(INA)第103(a)(1)和(3)条、8 U.S.C. 1103(a)(1)、(3)条,部长有权管理和执行美国的移民与归化法律,包括发布其认为必要的法规。[135]《移民与国籍法》第214(a)条、8 U.S.C. 1184(a)条授权部长规定任何外国人作为非移民入境的时间和条件。
The Secretary has authority under Section 102 of the Homeland Security Act of 2002 (HSA) (Pub. L. 107-296, 116 Stat. 2135), 6 U.S.C. 112, and section 103(a)(1) and (3) of the Immigration and Nationality Act (INA), 8 U.S.C. 1103(a)(1), (3), to administer and enforce the immigration and naturalization laws of the United States, to include the issuance of regulations that he deems necessary.[135] Section 214(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1184(a), authorizes the Secretary to prescribe the time and conditions of admission of any alien as a nonimmigrant.
国土安全部还考虑了一种替代方案,即将一次性的70,000美元初始费用,在F-1学生升入更高教育层次后(例如,从学士后OPT升至硕士学位OPT)对每一次新的初始OPT期间再次适用。国土安全部欢迎公众就此潜在替代方案提出意见。
DHS has also considered, as an alternative, having the one-time initial fee of $70,000 apply again to any initial period of OPT following the F-1 student's change to a higher educational level ( e.g., to a post-bachelor's degree OPT and again to the student's master's degree OPT). DHS welcomes public comment on this potential alternative.
3. 本拟议规则适用的小实体数量的描述,以及在可行的情况下,对相关小实体数量的估计;或说明为何无法提供此类估计
3. A Description of, and, Where Feasible, an Estimate of, the Number of Small Entities to Which the Proposed Rule Will Apply, or an Explanation of Why No Such Estimate Is Available
本拟议规则所适用的小实体包括所有推荐F-1非移民从事OPT的、获得SEVP认证的小型机构。截至2026年6月,共有6,186所机构获得授权可招收F-1非移民学生。[136]
The small entities to which the proposed rule would apply include all small SEVP-certified institutions that recommend F-1 nonimmigrants for OPT. As of June 2026, there were 6,186 institutions authorized to enroll F-1 nonimmigrant students.[136]
为识别受本拟议规则影响的小型机构,国土安全部审查了2022年、2023年和2024年就业开始日期的OPT授权情况,并确定在该三年期间内至少获得过一次OPT推荐的、有资格招收F-1非移民学生的机构共2,478所。在这些机构中,国土安全部估计有1,044所为公立机构(第64592页),1,294所为私立非营利机构,140所为私立营利性实体。国土安全部假设所有1,044所公立机构均不属于小实体,因为它们由管辖人口超过50,000人的州运营。[137]国土安全部保守地假设所有1,294所私立非营利机构均属于小实体,因为它们在各自领域中不占主导地位。[138]
To identify small institutions impacted by the proposed rule, DHS reviewed authorizations of OPT with employment start dates in 2022, 2023, and 2024 and identified 2,478 institutions eligible to enroll F-1 nonimmigrants who had been recommended OPT at least once over the three-year period. Of these institutions, DHS estimates 1044 are ( printed page 64592) public, 1,294 are private non-profit, and 140 are private for-profit entities. DHS assumes that all 1,044 public institutions are not small as they are run by states whose jurisdictions are larger than 50,000 people.[137] DHS conservatively assumes that all 1,294 private non-profit institutions are small entities because they are non-dominant in their field.[138]
为确定剩余140所营利性机构中哪些被视为小实体,美国国土安全部（DHS）参照了以平均年收入表示的美国小企业管理局（SBA）规模标准。收入一般定义为企业的总收入或毛收入。SBA的《小企业规模标准表》提供了北美行业分类系统（NAICS）所有行业部分的企业规模标准。[139] DHS将机构在学生与交流访问者信息系统（SEVIS）中提供的有关其所从事学习项目的信息,与相应的六位数NAICS行业说明进行了匹配。NAICS是用于对企业机构进行分类的标准分类法,以便收集、分析和发布与美国经济有关的统计数据。DHS发现,在140所营利性机构中,有95所的收入低于按其行业划分的SBA小企业规模标准。表11列出了按行业划分的小型SEVP认证营利性机构数量的明细情况。
To determine which of the remaining 140 for-profit institutions are considered a small entity, DHS referenced the SBA size standards represented by average annual receipts. Receipts are generally defined as a firm's total income or gross income. SBA's Table of Small Business Size Standards provides business size standards for all sections of the North American Industry Classification System (NAICS) for industries.[139] DHS matched information provided by the institutions in SEVIS regarding what programs of study they are engaged in with an appropriate six-digit NAICS industry description. NAICS is the standard classification used to categorize business establishments for the purpose of collecting, analyzing, and publishing statistical data related to the U.S. economy. DHS found that the revenue of 95 of the 140 for-profit institutions fell below the SBA size standard of a small business, according to their industry. Table 11 shows a breakdown of the number of small for-profit SEVP-certified institutions by industry.
DHS通过将食宿和学费的大致年度费用乘以学生总数的平均数（根据各机构向学生与交流访问者项目（SEVP）提供的数据）,估算了每所私立机构的年收入。DHS承认,这种估算收入的方法可能无法完整反映机构的收入状况,机构收入还可能包括私人捐助、捐赠基金或其他来源的款项。
DHS estimated each of the private institution's annual receipts by multiplying the approximate annual cost of room, board, and tuition by the average number of total students, based on data provided by the institutions to SEVP. DHS acknowledges that this method of estimating receipts may be an incomplete account of an institution's income, which may also include contributions from private individuals, endowments, or other sources.
表12按机构类型概述了获准招收F-1非移民学生的SEVP认证机构及小实体的数量。
Table 12 shows a summary, by institution type, of the number of SEVP-certified institutions authorized to enroll F-1 nonimmigrants and small entities.
（印刷页第64593页）
( printed page 64593)
4. 拟议规则预计产生的报告、记录保存和其他合规要求的说明,包括可能受该要求约束的小实体类别的估算,以及编制报告或记录所需的专业技能类型
4. A Description of the Projected Reporting, Recordkeeping, and Other Compliance Requirements of the Proposed Rule, Including an Estimate of the Classes of Small Entities That Would Be Subject to the Requirement and the Types of Professional Skills Necessary for Preparation of the Report or Record
DHS预计,招收参与选择性实习训练（OPT）的F-1非移民学生的小型机构将产生熟悉规则、付款处理和缴纳费用等方面的费用。DHS将此类机构称为受影响小型机构。每所受影响小型机构的费用将因参与OPT的在读学生人数而有所不同。
DHS anticipates that small institutions enrolling F-1 nonimmigrant students participating in OPT would incur costs from familiarization, payment processing, and payment of the fee. DHS refers to these institutions as small affected institutions. The cost would vary at each small affected institution depending on the number of enrolled students who participate in OPT.
为确保遵守拟议规则,DHS预计所有受影响小型机构都将产生熟悉规则的费用。为估算指定学校官员（DSO）相关的具体费用,DHS将熟悉/适应规则的总负担估计值（65小时）乘以DSO的综合工资率（每小时50.14美元[140]）,再乘以每所小型机构的DSO人数。对于机构行政管理人员的费用,DHS将相同的熟悉/适应规则总负担估计值（65小时）乘以机构行政管理人员的综合工资率（每小时88.83美元[141]）,再乘以每所机构中需要熟悉该规则的估计行政管理人员人数（5人）。将这些费用相加,得出每所机构的熟悉规则总费用,然后除以收入,以确定对小型机构的影响程度。在受影响的小型机构中,88.8%的机构因熟悉规则所产生的影响低于其所报收入的1%,如下表13所示。
To ensure compliance with the proposed rule, DHS expects that all small affected institutions would incur familiarization costs. To estimate the DSO-specific costs, DHS multiplied the total familiarization/adaptation burden estimate (65 hours) by the loaded wage rate for DSOs ($50.14 [140] ) and by the number of DSOs per small institution. For the institution administrator costs, DHS multiplied the same total familiarization/adaptation burden estimate (65 hours) by the loaded wage rate for institution administrators ($88.83 [141] ) and by an estimated number of 5 administrators required to familiarize themselves with the rule per institution. These costs were summed to a total familiarization cost per institution and then divided by the revenue to determine impact to the small institutions. Of the small institutions affected, 88.8 percent have effects for familiarization smaller than 1 percent of their reported revenue, as shown in Table 13 below.
为评估拟议规则的潜在最大影响,国土安全部(DHS)使用了就业开始日期在2022年、2023年和2024日历年内的选择性实习培训(OPT)授权数据。国土安全部使用Python(一种解释型、面向对象的高级编程语言)来汇编OPT授权数据并分配费用数值。[142] 为确定初始费和后续费,国土安全部审查了每名F-1非移民学生的选择性实习培训情况,将最早日期的一次授权指定为70,000美元的初始费,并将其后的其他授权指定为30,000美元的后续费。所有(印刷页64594)STEM OPT授权均被指定为后续费数值,因为该项目是OPT的延伸,须以先前获得授权为前提。随后,国土安全部按机构逐年统计初始费和后续费的数量。国土安全部随后计算了基于2022至2024年授权数据,某机构在初始费和后续费两方面可能支付的最高年度金额。例如,若某机构在2022年的初始授权数量最高,而在2024年的后续授权数量最高,则分别选取各类别中数值最高的年份,并以此估算最高年度费用影响。该数据假定历史上的OPT参与趋势即便在新拟议的OPT费用实施后仍将延续。表14列示了各类学校的总体及平均最高费用影响。上文所述方法在相关表格中标注为“过渡后”。
To assess the potential maximum impact of the proposed rule, DHS utilized data on OPT authorizations with employment start dates in calendar years 2022, 2023, and 2024. DHS used Python, an interpreted, object-oriented, high level programming language, to compile OPT authorizations and assign fee values.[142] To determine initial and subsequent fees, DHS reviewed optional practical training for every F-1 nonimmigrant student and assigned an initial fee amount of $70,000 to the earliest dated instance and a subsequent $30,000 fee for additional instances. All ( printed page 64594) authorizations of STEM OPT were assigned the subsequent fee value as the program is an extension of OPT that necessitated a prior authorization. The number of initial and subsequent fees was then tallied for each year by institution. DHS then calculated the maximum annual amount that an institution would have paid based on 2022-2024 authorizations for both the initial and subsequent fee. For example, if an institution had the highest initial authorizations in 2022 and the highest sequential authorizations in 2024, the highest years are picked for each category and then used to estimate the maximum annual fee impact. This data assumes that the historical OPT participation trends would continue into the future, even with the new proposed fees for OPT. Table 14 shows the total and average maximum fee impact across types of schools. The methodology discussed above is labeled post-transition in the relevant tables.
国土安全部还对过渡期内的费用影响进行了建模,在该过渡期内,所有F-1非移民的推荐,无论是OPT还是STEM OPT推荐,均须缴纳70,000美元的初始费。若某F-1非移民此前尚未缴纳过OPT费用,则须缴纳70,000美元的初始费。国土安全部将分析分为两个阶段:过渡年度,即所有被推荐参加任何类型OPT的F-1非移民均须缴纳70,000美元初始费;以及过渡后期间,即首次OPT推荐须缴纳70,000美元初始费,而STEM OPT延期须缴纳30,000美元后续费。国土安全部根据2022至2024年的历史OPT授权数据,为每个机构的OPT和STEM OPT参与者中数量最多者分配70,000美元的费用。表14、表15和表17均包含过渡年度及过渡后年度的影响数据。
DHS also modeled fee impacts in the transition period when all F-1 nonimmigrants recommendations would be subject to the initial fee amount of $70,000 regardless of whether the recommendation is for OPT or STEM OPT. If an OPT fee has not previously been paid for an F-1 nonimmigrant, they are subject to the initial fee of $70,000. DHS divides the analysis into a transition year, when the initial fee of $70,000 must be paid for all F-1 nonimmigrants recommended for any type of OPT, and a post-transition period when the first OPT recommendation requires the initial fee of $70,000 and the STEM OPT extension requires the subsequent fee of $30,000. DHS assigned the $70,000 fee to the maximum number of OPT and STEM OPT participants for each institution based on the historic 2022-2024 OPT authorization data. Tables 14, 15, and 17 contain both transition year and post-transition annual impacts.
接下来,国土安全部将各机构的最高年度费用金额与其在学生及交流访问者信息系统(SEVIS)I-17表中报告的预计年度收入进行了比较。表15显示了按各机构估计费用成本占机构年度收入百分比划分的影响区间内,各档次小型机构的数量分布。在受影响的1,389所小型机构中,在过渡后期间,有622所机构所受影响将小于或等于其预计年度收入的1%。而有483所机构所受影响将在其预计年度收入的1%至5%之间,104所机构所受影响将在5%至10%之间,82所机构所受影响将在10%至20%之间,98所机构所受影响将大于20%。
Next, DHS compared the institution's maximum annual fee amount to their estimated annual revenue as reported on their I-17 in SEVIS. Table 15 shows the number of small institutions within the stratified impact range of each institution's estimated fee costs as a percentage of the institution's annual revenue. Of the 1,389 small affected institutions, under the post transition period, 622 would experience an impact less than or equal to 1 percent of their estimated annual revenue as a result of the fee. While 483 would experience an impact between 1 percent and 5 percent of their estimated annual revenue, 104 would experience an impact between 5 percent and 10 percent, 82 would experience an impact between 10 percent and 20 percent, and 98 would experience an impact greater than 20 percent.
(印刷页64595)
( printed page 64595)
鉴于拟议费用金额巨大,国土安全部假定学校指定官员(DSO)可能无权授权向政府转账此类费用。因此,具有大额资金转账财务权限的更高级别机构管理人员将参与付款流程。国土安全部预计,费用的实际转账将需要DSO花费30分钟进行最终审查,并需要机构管理人员花费30分钟进行最终审查及费用转账。为估算这些成本,国土安全部假定DSO和管理人员对每份OPT申请的审批和处理各需30分钟。负担工时乘以DSO与管理人员的综合负荷工资率以及每个机构预期的OPT申请数量。表16的结果显示,由于审批和处理成本,几乎所有受影响的小型机构每年所受影响均将低于其年度收入的1%。
Due to the large monetary amount of the proposed fees, DHS assumes a DSO may not have the authority to authorize transmissions of the fees to the government. Instead, a higher-level institution administrator with financial authority to transmit large amounts of money would be involved in the payment process. DHS anticipates the transmission of the fee itself would require 30 minutes of DSO time for a final review and 30 minutes of time for an institution administrator to do a final review and fee transmission. To estimate these costs, DHS assumed 30 minutes of approval and processing of each OPT application for the DSO and an administrator. The burden hours were multiplied by the combined loaded wage rate for the DSO and administrator and the number of expected OPT applications per institution. The results in Table 16 show that nearly all small affected institutions would incur an annual impact of less than 1 percent of their annual revenue due to approval and processing costs.
为评估最大年度影响,国土安全部将费用、批准和处理负担以及熟悉程序的年度估计成本相加,并将每个机构的总成本与表17过渡年估计数据中该机构的收入进行比较。过渡后年度估计不包括熟悉程序成本,因为这些成本仅由拟议规则生效后首年的机构承担。在成本高于过渡后期间的过渡年度内,国土安全部发现548个小实体(39.5%)预计将承受低于收入1%的影响,505个小实体(36.4%)预计将承受收入1%至5%之间的影响,135个小实体(9.7%)预计将承受收入5%至10%之间的影响,85个小实体(6.1%)预计将承受收入10%至20%之间的影响,116个小实体(8.4%)预计将承受超过收入20%的影响。国土安全部估计,在1,389个小型机构总数中,841个(60.5%)预计将承受超过其估计收入1%的影响。
To assess maximum annual impact, DHS summed the annual estimated cost of the fees, the approval and processing burden, and familiarization and compared the total cost per institution to the institution's revenue in the transition year estimates of Table 17. The post-transition year estimates exclude the familiarization costs as these would only be borne by institutions in the initial year after the proposed rule would go into effect. During the transition year which has higher costs than the post-transition period, DHS found that 548 small entities (39.5 percent) would experience estimated impacts of less than 1 percent of revenue, 505 small entities (36.4 percent) would experience estimated impacts between 1 percent and 5 percent of revenue, 135 small entities (9.7 percent) would experience estimated impacts between 5 percent and 10 percent of revenue, 85 small entities (6.1 percent) would experience estimated impacts between 10 percent and 20 percent of revenue, and 116 small entities (8.4 percent) would experience estimated impacts greater than 20 percent of revenue. DHS estimates that out of 1,389 total small institutions, 841 (60.5 percent) would experience impacts greater than 1 percent of their estimated revenue.
(印刷页第64596页)
( printed page 64596)
国土安全部通过将估计成本与来自学费和其他学生相关来源的收入进行比较,评估了对受影响小型机构的影响。拟议规则并不排除机构通过雇主或组织等外部方的资金捐助来资助OPT费用。因此,对于能够获得额外资金的机构而言,以收入百分比表示的估计年度影响可能被高估。然而,一些受影响的小型机构可能无法获得此类资金,这可能导致因OPT参与度下降而产生的机会成本,包括入学率下降和收入减少。国土安全部无法准确预测由于缺乏资金将有多少学校或OPT参与者受到影响。在学校无法支付费用且OPT参与度下降的情况下,国土安全部假设未支付的费用可作为小型学校所面临机会成本的替代指标,这些成本可能包括入学率和学费收入的变化。
DHS assessed the impacts on small affected institutions by comparing estimated costs to revenue derived from tuition and other student-related sources. The proposed rule does not preclude institutions from funding the OPT fees through financial contributions from external parties, such as employers or organizations. As a result, the estimated annual impacts as a percentage of revenue may be overstated for institutions that can obtain additional funding. However, some small affected institutions may be unable to secure such funding, potentially resulting in opportunity costs from lost OPT participation, including declining enrollment and reduced revenue. DHS cannot accurately predict how many schools or OPT participants would be affected due to lack of funding. To the extent that schools are unable to pay the fees and OPT participation declines, DHS assumes that unpaid fees may serve as a proxy for the opportunity costs faced by small schools, which may include changes in enrollment and tuition revenue.
5. 在切实可行的范围内,识别所有可能与拟议规则重复、重叠或冲突的相关联邦规则
5. An Identification, to the Extent Practicable, of All Relevant Federal Rules Which May Duplicate, Overlap or Conflict With the Proposed Rule
国土安全部未识别出任何与本规则存在特定重复或重叠的规则。不存在与本处所提议规则相冲突的规则或拟议规则。可能存在旨在实现消除学生签证项目欺诈这一相同目标的规则,但本规则专门针对OPT项目内的欺诈行为,因此与其他规则并不重复。
DHS does not identify any rules that are specifically duplicative or overlap with this rule. There are no rules or proposed rules that conflict with the rule proposed here. There may be rules that seek the same benefit of eliminating fraud within the student visa program, but this rule targets fraud specifically within the OPT program, and therefore is not duplicative of other rules.
6. 机构为按照适用法规所述目标最大限度减少对小实体的重大经济影响而采取的措施说明,包括选择拟议规则中所采纳替代方案的事实、政策和法律理由说明,以及机构所考虑的、影响对小实体影响的其他每一项重大替代方案被否决的原因说明
6. Description of the Steps the Agency Has Taken To Minimize the Significant Economic Impact on Small Entities Consistent With the Stated Objectives of Applicable Statutes, Including a Statement of Factual, Policy, and Legal Reasons for Selecting the Alternative Adopted in the Proposed Rule and Why Each One of the Other Significant Alternatives to the Rule Considered by the Agency That Affect the Impact on Small Entities Was Rejected
国土安全部考虑了一项不采取行动的替代方案,根据该方案,机构可继续推荐F-1非移民学生从事OPT而无需支付额外费用。国土安全部认定,该替代方案无法为学校提供足够的问责机制以确保OPT符合所有监管要求。国土安全部假设,针对每项OPT推荐收取费用将激励学校更加尽职地审查每项OPT推荐的必要性,评估OPT对非移民的益处,并确保合规。
DHS considered a no-action alternative, under which institutions could continue to recommend F-1 nonimmigrant students to engage in OPT without paying an additional fee. DHS determined that this alternative does not provide enough accountability for schools to ensure the OPT is in compliance with all regulatory requirements. DHS assumes that including a fee per OPT recommendation would provide incentive for schools to more diligently review the need for each OPT recommendation, assess the benefits of the OPT to the nonimmigrant, and ensure compliance.
国土安全部考虑了一项替代方案,即将小型教育机构从拟议规则的费用要求中予以豁免。国土安全部估计,56%受监管的SEVP认证机构为小实体。这意味着,鉴于大多数为小实体,欺诈行为仍可能持续存在。
DHS considered an alternative that exempts small educational institutions from the fee requirements in the proposed rule. DHS estimates that 56 percent of regulated SEVP-certified institutions are small entities. This would mean that fraud could still continue given the majority are small entities.
作为对上述方法的替代方案,国土安全部还考虑了将70,000美元的一次性初始费用再次适用于F-1学生升入更高教育层次后任何初始OPT期间的方案(例如,适用于学士后学位OPT,以及再次适用于该学生硕士学位OPT)。国土安全部欢迎公众就此潜在替代方案提出意见。
DHS has also considered, as an alternative to the approach described above, having the one-time initial fee of $70,000 apply again to any initial period of OPT following the F-1 student's change to a higher educational level ( e.g., to a post-bachelor's degree OPT and again to the student's master's degree OPT). DHS welcomes public comment on this potential alternative.
国土安全部(DHS)还考虑了更为复杂的监管替代方案,例如扩大实地考察范围、增加报告要求、增设审批程序或实施更严格的程序性规定。国土安全部拒绝了这些替代方案,因为其认为提议对参加选择性实习培训(OPT)项目收取费用,能够以对政府最小的行政负担,提供一种立即有效的方式来加强项目的完整性。欲了解更多讨论,请参阅第IV.C节。(印刷页第64597页)
DHS also considered more complex regulatory alternatives, such as expanded site visits, increased reporting requirements, additional processes, or stricter procedural mandates. DHS rejected these alternatives because it found proposing a fee for participation in the OPT program would offer an immediate and effective means to strengthen program integrity with minimal administrative burdens on the government. For further discussion, see IV.C. ( printed page 64597)
C. 1996年《小企业监管公平法》
C. Small Business Regulatory Enforcement Fairness Act of 1996
根据1996年《小企业监管公平法》(公法104-121)第213(a)条的规定,我们希望协助小实体理解本拟议规则,以便其能够更好地评估该规则对其产生的影响并参与规则制定。如果本拟议规则会影响贵小企业、组织或政府管辖机构,且贵方对其规定或合规选项存有疑问,请使用上文“联系方式”部分提供的联系信息联系美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)。
Under section 213(a) of the Small Business Regulatory Enforcement Fairness Act of 1996, Public Law 104-121, we want to assist small entities in understanding this proposed rule so that they can better evaluate its effects on them and participate in the rulemaking. If the proposed rule would affect your small business, organization, or governmental jurisdiction and you have questions concerning its provisions or options for compliance, please consult ICE using the contact information provided in the FOR FURTHER INFORMATION CONTACT section above.
D. 《无资金拨款授权改革法》
D. Unfunded Mandates Reform Act
《1995年无资金拨款授权改革法》(2 U.S.C. 1531-1538)要求联邦机构评估其自由裁量监管行动的影响。具体而言,该法针对的是可能导致州、地方或部落政府合计支出,或私营部门支出达到或超过1亿美元(经通胀调整)的任何一年的行动。尽管本拟议规则预计不会导致此类支出,国土安全部仍在本序言的其他部分讨论了本规则的影响。
The Unfunded Mandates Reform Act of 1995 (2 U.S.C. 1531-1538) requires federal agencies to assess the effects of their discretionary regulatory actions. In particular, the Act addresses actions that may result in the expenditure by a State, local, or tribal government, in the aggregate, or by the private sector of $100,000,000 (adjusted for inflation) or more in any year. Though this proposed rule would not result in such an expenditure, DHS does discuss the effects of this rule elsewhere in this preamble.
1995年《无资金拨款授权改革法》(UMRA)的目的之一是遏制向州、地方和部落政府施加无资金拨款的联邦强制性规定的做法。UMRA第二章要求每个联邦机构就拟议或最终机构规则中可能导致州、地方和部落政府合计,或私营部门在任何一年内支出1亿美元或以上(每年根据通胀进行调整)的任何联邦强制性规定的影响,编写书面评估声明。按照所有城市消费者消费价格指数计算,1995年1亿美元的等值金额调整至2025年水平为2.1136亿美元。本规则在实施的第一年即超过该1亿美元(经通胀调整)的支出门槛,因此国土安全部提供本项UMRA分析。
The Unfunded Mandate Reform Act of 1995 (UMRA) is intended, among other things, to curb the practice of imposing unfunded Federal mandates on state, local, and tribal governments. Title II of UMRA requires each Federal agency to prepare a written statement assessing the effects of any Federal mandate in a proposed or final agency rule that may result in a $100 million or more expenditure (adjusted annually for inflation) in any one year by state, local, and tribal governments, in the aggregate, or by the private sector. The value equivalent of $100 million in 1995 adjusted for inflation to 2025 levels by the Consumer Price Index for All Urban Consumers is $211.36 million. This rule exceeds the $100 million expenditure threshold in the first year of implementation (adjusted for inflation), and therefore DHS is providing this UMRA analysis.
1. 明确本规则所依据颁布的联邦法律条款
1. An Identification of the Provision of Federal Law Under Which the Rule Is Being Promulgated
根据2002年《国土安全法》(公法107-296,116 Stat. 2135)第102条、6 U.S.C. 112,以及《移民与国籍法》(INA)第103(a)(1)和(3)条、8 U.S.C. 1103(a)(1)、(3)的规定,部长有权管理和执行美国的移民与归化法律,包括发布其认为必要的法规。INA第214(a)条、8 U.S.C. 1184(a)授权部长规定任何外国人作为非移民入境的时间和条件。
The Secretary has authority under Section 102 of the Homeland Security Act of 2002 (Pub. L. 107-296, 116 Stat. 2135), 6 U.S.C. 112, and section 103(a)(1) and (3) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1103(a)(1), (3), to administer and enforce the immigration and naturalization laws of the United States, to include the issuance of regulations that he deems necessary. Section 214(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1184(a), authorizes the Secretary to prescribe the time and conditions of admission of any alien as a nonimmigrant.
2. 对联邦强制性规定预期成本和收益的定性与定量评估,包括对州、地方和部落政府或私营部门的成本和收益,以及该联邦强制性规定对健康、安全和自然环境的影响
2. A Qualitative and Quantitative Assessment of the Anticipated Costs and Benefits of the Federal Mandate, Including the Costs and Benefits to State, Local, and Tribal Governments or the Private Sector, as Well as the Effect of the Federal Mandate on Health, Safety, and the Natural Environment
本规则所涉及的受影响人群是负责管理申请参加OPT的F-1非移民学生的经SEVP认证的教育机构。与本规则相关的机构主要成本包括OPT申请费、第一年产生的熟悉和适应负担,以及审查申请和处理OPT申请付款所需的额外时间。熟悉和适应负担适用于全部受影响人群,但每个机构的负担程度不同,取决于熟悉本规则的指定学校官员(DSO)和管理人员的数量。国土安全部(DHS)预计DSO和管理人员将承担熟悉和适应负担所产生的成本,并对其OPT批准流程进行调整,以确保所缴纳的费用对应合法的OPT申请。选择提交OPT推荐的机构将承担每次申请70,000美元或30,000美元的费用。机构可以自行选择如何在收取费用的情况下批准OPT推荐,因此,与批准和付款相关的费用及所有成本将取决于每个机构的OPT申请数量。机构可能选择采用更严格的OPT流程,以确保所有OPT机会均属合法,从而导致各机构成本存在差异。
The affected population of the rule is the SEVP-certified educational institutions responsible for the F-1 nonimmigrant students who are applying to participate in OPT. The major costs to the institutions associated with this rule are the OPT application fees, the familiarization and adaptation burden incurred in year 1, and additional time to review applications and process payment for OPT applications. The familiarization and adaptation burden applies to the full population, although in different amounts per institution, depending on the number of DSOs and administrators who become familiarized with the rule. DHS expects DSOs and administrators to incur costs for familiarization and adaptation burden and make changes to their OPT approval processes to ensure the fees paid are legitimate requests for OPT. Institutions which choose to submit OPT recommendations would incur the fees, either $70,000 or $30,000 per filing. Institutions can choose how they would approve OPT recommendations with the fee; therefore, the fee and all costs associated with approval and payment would be dependent on each institution's number of OPT filings. Institutions may choose to have more stringent OPT processes to ensure all OPT opportunities are legitimate, leading to variable costs per institution.
国土安全部认为本规则不会对健康或安全产生影响。本规则对环境问题的影响将在本拟议规则第七节I分节“国家环境政策法(NEPA)审查”中作更全面的讨论。
DHS does not believe that this rule would have impacts on health or safety. The impact of this rule on environmental issues is discussed more fully in Review under the National Environmental Policy Act (NEPA), Section VII, subpart I of this proposed rule.
3. 本机构就联邦强制令的未来合规成本,以及该联邦强制令对国家特定地区或特定州、地方、部落政府、城市或农村或其他类型社区,或私营部门特定部分可能产生的不成比例预算影响所作的估算(如该机构认定作出准确估算在合理可行范围内,并在该范围内作出估算)
3. Estimates by the Agency, if and to the Extent That the Agency Determines That Accurate Estimates Are Reasonably Feasible of Future Compliance Costs of the Federal Mandate and Any Disproportionate Budgetary Effects of the Federal Mandate Upon Any Particular Regions of the Nation or Particular State, Local, or Tribal Governments, Urban or Rural or Other Types of Communities, or Particular Segments of the Private Sector
国土安全部已就本拟议规则各项条款中可能间接触发联邦强制令的主要条款提供了合规成本,并已在《初步监管灵活性分析》(IRFA)中予以说明。国土安全部重申,州政府及私营部门实体自行作出是否投入资金以参与OPT项目下外国非移民事务的成本效益决策,以及是否需要额外资金来源以利用该项目。
DHS has provided compliance costs for the main provisions that may indirectly trigger Federal mandates of each provision of this proposed rule as well as in the IRFA. DHS reiterates that state and private sector entities make the cost-benefit decisions of whether to expend finances to engage with foreign nonimmigrants participating in the OPT program, and if additional funding sources are needed to utilize this program.
4. 本机构就本规则对国民经济的影响(例如对生产率、经济增长、充分就业、创造生产性就业机会以及美国商品和服务的国际竞争力的影响)所作的估算(如该机构自行裁量认定作出准确估算在合理可行范围内,且该影响具有相关性和实质性)
4. Estimates by the Agency of the Effect on the National Economy, Such as the Effect on Productivity, Economic Growth, Full Employment, Creation of Productive Jobs, and International Competitiveness of United States Goods and Services, if and to the Extent That the Agency in Its Sole Discretion Determines That Accurate Estimates Are Reasonably Feasible and That Such Effect Is Relevant and Material
国土安全部已在本规则的《监管影响分析》中对本规则对经济的影响进行了讨论。详见本拟议规则第七节B分节。
DHS has provided discussions of the effect of this rule on the economy in the Regulatory Impact Analysis of this rule. See Section VII, subpart B of the proposed rule.
5. 关于本机构根据第204条就受影响的州、地方和部落政府民选代表进行事先磋商情况的说明;州、地方或部落政府以口头或书面形式向本机构提出的意见和关切的摘要;以及本机构对这些意见和关切所作评估的摘要
5. A Description of the Extent of the Agency's Prior Consultation With Elected Representatives (Under Section 204) of the Affected State, Local, and Tribal Governments; a Summary of the Comments and Concerns That Were Presented by State, Local, or Tribal Governments Either Orally or in Writing to the Agency; and a Summary of the Agency's Evaluation of Those Comments and Concerns
国土安全部未就本规则与受影响的州、地方和部落政府的民选代表进行磋商。国土安全部正就本拟议规则接受公众意见,供相关实体表达支持、关切,或就本拟议规则的成本和效益提供补充信息。
DHS did not consult with elected representatives of the affected State, local, and tribal governments. DHS is accepting public comments on the proposed rule for entities to express support, concern, or provide additional information about the costs and benefits of the proposed rule.
E. 《文书削减法》,信息收集
E. Paperwork Reduction Act—Collection of Information
根据1995年《文书削减法》(Public Law 104-13, 109 Stat. 163 (1995),简称PRA),各部门须就规则中固有的任何报告或(印刷版第64598页)记录保存要求,提交管理和预算办公室(OMB)审查和批准。国土安全部正在修订OMB管制编号为1653-0038的信息收集项目,以配合本规则制定行动提出非实质性修改。
Under the Paperwork Reduction Act of 1995, Public Law 104-13, 109 Stat. 163 (1995) (PRA), all Departments are required to submit to OMB, for review and approval, any reporting or ( printed page 64598) recordkeeping requirements inherent in a rule. DHS is revising an information collection under OMB Control No. 1653-0038 to propose non-substantive edits in association with this rulemaking action.
国土安全部邀请公众及其他联邦机构就拟议信息收集的影响发表意见。根据《文书削减法》的要求,本信息收集通知已在《联邦公报》上公布,以征求有关拟议信息收集文件修改内容的意见。
DHS invites the general public and other federal agencies to comment on the impact of the proposed collection of information. In accordance with the PRA, the information collection notice is published in the Federal Register to obtain comments regarding the proposed edits to the information collection instrument.
欢迎提交意见,自本拟议规则发布之日起60天内接受意见。所有提交的材料必须在信函正文中包含OMB控制编号1653-0038及机构名称。为避免重复提交,请仅使用本规则“地址”和“公众参与”部分下列明的一种方式提交意见。对本信息收集的意见应针对以下四点中的一点或多点:
Comments are encouraged and will be accepted for 60 days from the publication date of the proposed rule. All submissions received must include the OMB Control Number 1653-0038 in the body of the letter and the agency name. To avoid duplicate submissions, please use only one of the methods under the ADDRESSES and Public Participation section of this rule to submit comments. Comments on this information collection should address one or more of the following four points:
(1)评估收集该信息对于本机构正确履行职能是否必要,包括该信息是否具有实际效用;
(1) Evaluate whether the collection of information is necessary for the proper performance of the functions of the agency, including whether the information will have practical utility;
(2)评估本机构对收集该信息所需负担的估计是否准确,包括所使用方法和假设的有效性;
(2) Evaluate the accuracy of the agency's estimate of the burden of the collection of information, including the validity of the methodology and assumptions used;
(3)提高拟收集信息的质量、效用和明确性;以及
(3) Enhance the quality, utility, and clarity of the information to be collected; and
(4)尽量减轻应答者在信息收集方面的负担,包括通过使用适当的自动化、电子化、机械化或其他技术性收集方式,或其他形式的信息技术,例如允许以电子方式提交答复。
(4) Minimize the burden of the collection of information on those who are to respond, including through the use of appropriate automated, electronic, mechanical, or other technological collection techniques or other forms of information technology, e.g., permitting electronic submission of responses.
学生与交流访问者信息系统(SEVIS)
The Student and Exchange Visitor Information System (SEVIS)
信息收集概述
Overview of Information Collection
(1)信息收集类型:对现已获批准的信息收集的修订。
(1) Type of Information Collection: Revision of a Currently Approved Collection.
(2)表格/收集项目名称:学生与交流访问者信息系统(SEVIS)。
(2) Title of the Form/Collection: The Student and Exchange Visitor Information System (SEVIS).
(3)机构表格编号(如有)及负责该收集项目的国土安全部(DHS)相应部门:不适用表格编号。信息将由移民及海关执法局(ICE)学生与交流访问者项目(SEVP)负责收集。
(3) Agency form number, if any, and the applicable component of the DHS sponsoring the collection: Form number not applicable. Information to be collected by ICE SEVP.
(4)将被要求或需要作答的受影响公众,以及简要摘要:经SEVP认证机构的指定学校官员(DSO)将负责在SEVIS中更新与适用OPT费用付款确认相关的信息。
(4) Affected public who will be asked or required to respond, as well as a brief abstract: DSOs at SEVP-certified institutions will be responsible for updating SEVIS with information relating to payment confirmation of applicable OPT fees.
(5)应答人总数的估计及平均每位应答人作答所需时间的估计:本信息收集的估计应答人总数为10,800人,代表DSO人数,基于估计的OPT费用数量,总应答次数为226,200次。[143]每次应答的估计小时负担为0.083小时(5分钟)。
(5) An estimate of the total number of respondents and the amount of time estimated for an average respondent to respond: The estimated total number of respondents for this information collection is 10,800, which represents the number of DSOs, and the total number of responses is 226,200 based on the estimated number of OPT fees.[143] The estimated hour burden per response is .083 hours (5 minutes).
(6)与该收集项目相关的公众总负担(以小时计)的估计:与此信息收集相关的估计年度总负担小时数为18,850小时。
(6) An estimate of the total public burden (in hours) associated with the collection: The total estimated annual burden associated with this collection of information in hours is 18,850.
(7)与该收集项目相关的公众总负担(以费用计)的估计:与此信息收集相关的估计年度总费用负担为945,138美元。
(7) An estimate of the total public burden (in cost) associated with the collection: The estimated total annual cost burden associated with this collection of information is $945,138.
F. 第13132号行政命令:联邦制
F. Executive Order 13132: Federalism
本拟议规则不会对各州、国家政府与各州之间的关系,或政府各级之间权力与责任的分配产生实质性直接影响。国土安全部(DHS)预计本拟议规则不会对州和地方政府施加实质性直接合规成本,也不会优先于州法律。因此,根据第13132号行政命令《联邦制》第6条的规定,本拟议规则被确定不具有足以需要编写联邦制问题影响摘要说明的联邦制影响。
This proposed rule would not have substantial direct effects on the States, on the relationship between the National Government and the States, or on the distribution of power and responsibilities among the various levels of government. DHS does not expect that this proposed rule would impose substantial direct compliance costs on State and local governments or preempt State law. Therefore, in accordance with section 6 of Executive Order 13132, Federalism, it is determined that this proposed rule does not have sufficient federalism implications to warrant the preparation of a federalism summary impact statement.
G. 第12988号行政命令:民事司法改革
G. Executive Order 12988: Civil Justice Reform
本拟议规则符合第12988号行政命令《民事司法改革》第3(a)条和第3(b)(2)条中规定的适用标准,旨在消除起草错误和含糊之处,减少诉讼,为受影响的行为提供明确的法律标准,并促进简化和减轻负担。
This proposed rule meets applicable standards set forth in sections 3(a) and 3(b)(2) of Executive Order 12988, Civil Justice Reform, to eliminate drafting errors and ambiguity, minimize litigation, provide a clear legal standard for affected conduct, and promote simplification and burden reduction.
H. 第13211号行政命令:涉及对能源供应、分配或使用产生重大影响的法规的行动
H. Executive Order 13211: Actions Concerning Regulations That Significantly Affect Energy Supply, Distribution, or Use
国土安全部(DHS)已根据第13211号行政命令《涉及对能源供应、分配或使用产生重大影响的法规的行动》对本拟议规则进行了分析。国土安全部已确定,根据该命令,本规则并非“重大能源行动”,因为尽管其根据第12866号行政命令属于“重大监管行动”,但不太可能对能源的供应、分配或使用产生重大不利影响。
DHS has analyzed this proposed rule under Executive Order 13211, Actions Concerning Regulations That Significantly Affect Energy Supply, Distribution, or Use. DHS has determined that it is not a “significant energy action” under that order because it is a “significant regulatory action” under Executive Order 12866 but is not likely to have a significant adverse effect on the supply, distribution, or use of energy.
I. 《国家环境政策法》(NEPA)
I. National Environmental Policy Act (NEPA)
国土安全部管理指令(MD)023-01第01次修订版及说明手册(IM)023-01-001-01第01次修订版确立了国土安全部及其各部门用于遵守1969年《国家环境政策法》(NEPA)(42 U.S.C. 4321-4375)的政策和程序。
DHS Management Directive (MD) 023-01 Rev. 01 and Instruction Manual (IM) 023-01-001-01 Rev. 01 establish the policy and procedures that DHS and its Components use to comply with the National Environmental Policy Act of 1969 (NEPA), 42 U.S.C. 4321-4375.
《国家环境政策法》(NEPA)允许联邦机构设立若干行动类别(“分类排除”),根据经验表明,这些行动类别单独或累积均不会对人类环境产生重大影响,因此不需要编制环境评估(EA)或环境影响报告书(EIS)。[144] 参见42 U.S.C. 4336(a)(2)、4336e(1)。《操作手册》附录A表1列出了国土安全部(DHS)的分类排除。[145]
NEPA allows Federal agencies to establish categories of actions (“categorical exclusions”) that experience has shown do not, individually or cumulatively, have a significant effect on the human environment and, therefore, do not require an environmental assessment (EA) or environmental impact statement (EIS). [144] See42 U.S.C. 4336(a)(2), 4336e(1). The Instruction Manual, Appendix A, Table 1, lists the DHS Categorical Exclusions.[145]
根据国土安全部《国家环境政策法》实施程序,一项行动要获得分类排除,必须同时满足以下三个条件:(1) 该行动整体明确符合一项或多项分类排除;(2) 该行动不是更大行动的一部分;(3) 不存在可能导致重大环境影响的特殊情况。[146] 国土安全部拟修订其法规,为“选择性实习培训”(OPT)设定费用,以加强移民计划的完整性,打击欺诈行为,并保护美国工人。
Under DHS NEPA implementing procedures, for an action to be categorically excluded, it must satisfy each of the following three conditions: (1) The entire action clearly fits within one or more of the categorical exclusions; (2) the action is not a piece of a larger action; and (3) no extraordinary circumstances exist that create the potential for a significant environmental effect.[146] DHS proposes to amend its regulations to set fees for OPT, to strengthen the integrity of the immigration program, address fraud and protect U.S. workers.
国土安全部已根据《管理指令》MD 023-01 Rev. 01 及《操作手册》IM 023-01-001-01 Rev. 01 对本拟议规则进行了分析。国土安全部确定,本拟议规则制定行动属于单独或累积均不会对人类环境产生重大影响的一类行动。本拟议规则完全符合《操作手册》IM 023-01-001-01 Rev. 01 附录A表1第A3(d)项所列的分类排除:“在不改变现有法规环境影响的情况下,对其进行解释或修订的规则制定行为。”本拟议规则不是更大行动的一部分。本拟议规则不存在可能导致重大环境影响的特殊情况。(printed page 64599)因此,本拟议规则被归类排除,无需进一步开展《国家环境政策法》审查。
DHS has analyzed this proposed rule under MD 023-01 Rev. 01 and IM 023-01-001-01 Rev. 01. DHS has determined that this proposed rulemaking action is one of a category of actions that do not individually or cumulatively have a significant effect on the human environment. This proposed rule completely fits within the Categorical Exclusion found in IM 023-01-001-01 Rev. 01, Appendix A, Table 1, number A3(d): “Promulgation of rules. that interpret or amend an existing regulation without changing its environmental effect.” This proposed rule is not part of a larger action. This proposed rule presents no extraordinary ( printed page 64599) circumstances, creating the potential for significant environmental effects. Therefore, this proposed rule is categorically excluded from further NEPA review.
国土安全部欢迎任何可能有助于发现本拟议规则存在重大环境影响的意见或信息。
DHS seeks any comments or information that may lead to the discovery of any significant environmental effects from this proposed rule.
J. 第13175号行政命令:与印第安部落政府的磋商与协调
J. Executive Order 13175: Consultation and Coordination With Indian Tribal Governments
根据第13175号行政命令《与印第安部落政府的磋商与协调》,本拟议规则不涉及部落事项,因为它不会对一个或多个印第安部落、联邦政府与印第安部落之间的关系,或联邦政府与印第安部落之间权力和责任的分配产生实质性直接影响。
This proposed rule does not have tribal implications under Executive Order 13175, Consultation and Coordination with Indian Tribal Governments, because it would not have a substantial direct effect on one or more Indian tribes, on the relationship between the Federal Government and Indian tribes, or on the distribution of power and responsibilities between the Federal Government and Indian tribes.
K. 第12630号行政命令:政府行为与对宪法保护财产权的干预
K. Executive Order 12630: Governmental Actions and Interference With Constitutionally Protected Property Rights
根据第12630号行政命令《政府行为与对宪法保护财产权的干预》,本拟议规则不会导致对私有财产的征用,也不会产生征用方面的影响。
This proposed rule would not cause a taking of private property or otherwise have taking implications under Executive Order 12630, Governmental Actions and Interference with Constitutionally Protected Property Rights.
L. 第13045号行政命令:保护儿童免受环境健康风险和安全风险
L. Executive Order 13045: Protection of Children From Environmental Health Risks and Safety Risks
第13045号行政命令《保护儿童免受环境健康风险和安全风险》要求各机构考虑可能对儿童产生不成比例影响的环境健康风险或安全风险。国土安全部已对本拟议规则进行审查,并确定尽管本规则属于具有经济重大影响的规则,但它不会产生可能对儿童造成不成比例影响的环境健康风险或安全风险。因此,国土安全部未依据本行政命令编制相关声明。
Executive Order 13045, Protection of Children from Environmental Health Risks and Safety Risks, requires agencies to consider the impacts of environmental health risk or safety risk that may disproportionately affect children. DHS has reviewed this proposed rule and determined that even though this rule is an economically significant rule, it would not create an environmental risk to health or risk to safety that might disproportionately affect children. Therefore, DHS has not prepared a statement under this executive order.
M. 《国家技术转移与进步法》
M. National Technology Transfer and Advancement Act
1995年《国家技术转移与进步法》(15 U.S.C. 272 note)要求各机构在其监管活动中使用自愿性共识标准,除非该机构通过管理和预算办公室向国会说明使用这些标准为何与适用法律不符或在其他方面不可行。自愿性共识标准是指由自愿性共识标准制定机构制定或采纳的技术标准(例如,材料、性能、设计或操作的规格;测试方法;抽样程序;以及相关的管理体系实践)。本拟议规则未使用技术标准。因此,我们未考虑使用自愿性共识标准的问题。
The National Technology Transfer and Advancement Act of 1995 (15 U.S.C. 272 note) directs agencies to use voluntary consensus standards in their regulatory activities unless the agency provides Congress, through the Office of Management and Budget, with an explanation of why using these standards would be inconsistent with applicable law or otherwise impracticable. Voluntary consensus standards are technical standards ( e.g., specifications of materials, performance, design, or operation; test methods; sampling procedures; and related management systems practices) that are developed or adopted by voluntary consensus standards bodies. This proposed rule does not use technical standards. Therefore, we did not consider the use of voluntary consensus standards.
N. 家庭评估
N. Family Assessment
国土安全部(DHS)已确定,根据1999年综合统一紧急补充拨款法案(公法105-277号,《美国法典》第112卷第2681页)中颁布的《财政部和联邦政府总体拨款法案》第654条的含义,该拟议行动不会影响家庭福祉。
DHS has determined that this proposed action would not affect family well-being within the meaning of section 654 of the Treasury and General Government Appropriations Act, enacted as part of the Omnibus Consolidated and Emergency Supplemental Appropriations Act of 1999 (Pub. L. 105-277, 112 Stat. 2681).

第214.2条
§ 214.2
入境、延期及维持身份的特殊要求。
Special requirements for admission, extension, and maintenance of status.

(i) 学生责任。学生必须通过向其指定学校官员(DSO)申请OPT推荐,启动OPT申请程序。在作出推荐后,DSO应向学生提供一份已签署的I-20表,注明该推荐以及根据8 CFR 214.13(i)适用的初始OPT费用或后续OPT费用的缴费信息。
(i) Student responsibilities. A student must initiate the OPT application process by requesting a recommendation for OPT from his or her DSO. Upon making the recommendation, the DSO will provide the student a signed Form I-20 indicating the recommendation and payment information of the initial OPT fee or subsequent OPT fee, as applicable pursuant to 8 CFR 214.13(i).
(ii) DSO的附加责任。学生必须获得其DSO的推荐才能申请OPT。在DSO向学生签发OPT推荐之前,DSO必须确认学校已按照第214.13(i)(1)或(2)条的规定为该学生缴纳了适用的OPT费用,然后在SEVIS系统中更新OPT费用缴纳信息。DSO自此承担维护该学生在整个经批准OPT期间SEVIS记录的额外责任,并应符合本条第(f)(12)段的规定。
(ii) Additional DSO responsibilities. A student must have a recommendation from his or her DSO in order to apply for OPT. Prior to the DSO issuing a recommendation to a student for OPT, the DSO must confirm that the school has paid the applicable OPT fee for the student pursuant to § 214.13(i)(1) or (2) and then update SEVIS with the OPT fee payment information. The DSO assumes the added responsibility for maintaining the SEVIS record of that student for the entire period of authorized OPT, consistent with paragraph (f)(12) of this section.
* * * * *
* * * * *
(B) DSO必须在发出OPT推荐以及学生向USCIS申请就业授权之前,更新学生的SEVIS记录以确认教育机构已缴纳适用的OPT费用。DSO应在SEVIS中注明OPT就业是全职还是兼职,或者对于根据本条第(f)(10)(ii)(C)段申请24个月OPT延期推荐的学生,注明该OPT就业是否符合本条第(f)(10)(ii)(C)(8)段所述的最低工时要求,并在SEVIS中注明OPT的起止日期。
(B) The DSO must update the student's SEVIS record to confirm that the educational institution has paid the applicable OPT fee prior to issuing the DSO's recommendation for OPT and before the student can apply to USCIS for employment authorization. The DSO will indicate in SEVIS whether the OPT employment is to be full-time or part-time, or for a student seeking a recommendation for a 24-month OPT extension under paragraph (f)(10)(ii)(C) of this section whether the OPT employment meets the minimum hours requirements described in paragraph (f)(10)(ii)(C)( 8) of this section, and note in SEVIS the OPT start and end dates.
(C) DSO必须向学生提供一份已签署、注明日期的I-20表或后续表格,表明已推荐OPT,并确认教育机构已缴纳适用的OPT费用。
(C) The DSO must provide the student with a signed, dated Form I-20 or successor form indicating that OPT has been recommended and confirmation that the educational institution paid the applicable OPT fee.
* * * * *
* * * * *
(iii) 关于OPT就业授权申请的决定。美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)将根据DSO的推荐及其他资格考量因素,裁决学生提交的I-765表《就业授权申请书》或后续表格。如果DSO在学校缴纳所需OPT费用之前即发出推荐,或学校未能缴纳所有适用的OPT费用,则USCIS不得向任何学生授予就业授权,此规定应符合本条第(f)(11)(ii)段以及第214.13(i)(1)和(2)条的要求。
(iii) Decision on application for OPT employment authorization. USCIS will adjudicate a student's Form I-765, Application for Employment Authorization or successor form on the basis of the DSO's recommendation and other eligibility considerations. USCIS may not grant employment authorization to any student where the DSO issued the recommendation prior to the school paying the required OPT fees or where the school has failed to pay all applicable OPT fees, consistent with paragraph (f)(11)(ii) of this section and § 214.13(i)(1) and (2).

(i) 选择性实习培训费用。关于选择性实习培训（OPT），适用以下规定：
(i) Optional practical training fees. For optional practical training (OPT), the following will apply:
(1) 学生及交流访问者计划（SEVP）认证的教育机构必须为每名F-1学生首次被推荐参加根据第214.2(f)(10)(ii)条进行的OPT支付一次性初始费用70,000美元。根据第214.2(f)(11)(ii)条的规定，此项付款必须在指定校方官员（DSO）推荐学生参加OPT之前完成。学生及交流访问者计划（SEVP）可酌情根据个案情况批准学校提出的退款申请，但前提是该F-1学生尚未获得与该笔退款申请相关的OPT就业授权文件。一旦F-1学生获得OPT就业授权文件，该费用在任何情况下均不予退还。DSO必须在请求退款之前在SEVIS系统中撤销该学生的OPT推荐。学生及交流访问者计划（SEVP）可能会在其网站https://studyinthestates.dhs.gov（或后续统一资源定位符）上发布关于费用退款裁定的进一步指导。退款申请的决定不受任何行政上诉程序的约束。
(1) An SEVP-certified educational institution must pay a one-time initial fee of $70,000 for each F-1 student the first time that the alien is recommended to participate in OPT under section 214.2(f)(10)(ii). This payment must be made prior to the DSO recommending a student for OPT, pursuant to section 214.2(f)(11)(ii). SEVP may exercise discretion to grant a refund request from a school on a case-by-case basis, provided that the F-1 student has not been issued the employment authorization document for OPT associated with the fee that is subject to the refund request. This fee will not be refunded under any circumstances once the F-1 student has been granted the employment authorization document for OPT. The DSO must remove the student's OPT recommendation in SEVIS before requesting a refund. SEVP may issue further guidance on fee refund adjudications on the SEVP website at https://studyinthestates.dhs.gov (or successor uniform resource locator). Refund request determinations are not subject to any administrative appeals.
(2) 如果某外国人此前已参加过OPT并已缴纳70,000美元的初始OPT费用，学生及交流访问者计划（SEVP）认证的教育机构在再次推荐该外国人根据第214.2(f)(10)(ii)条参加OPT之前，必须为每名F-1学生支付30,000美元的费用。根据第214.2(f)(11)(ii)条的规定，此项付款必须在DSO推荐学生参加OPT之前完成。学生及交流访问者计划（SEVP）可酌情根据个案情况批准学校提出的退款申请，但前提是该F-1学生尚未获得与该笔退款申请相关的后续OPT就业授权文件，或尚未获得OPT延期批准。一旦F-1学生获得该后续OPT或OPT延期的就业授权文件，该费用在任何情况下均不予退还。DSO必须在请求退款之前在SEVIS系统中撤销该学生的OPT推荐。学生及交流访问者计划（SEVP）可能会在其网站https://studyinthestates.dhs.gov（或后续统一资源定位符）上发布关于费用退款裁定的进一步指导。退款申请的决定不受任何行政上诉程序的约束。
(2) An SEVP-certified educational institution must pay the amount of $30,000 for each F-1 student prior to a subsequent recommendation of the alien to participate in OPT under section 214.2(f)(10)(ii) if the alien has previously participated in OPT and paid the initial OPT fee of $70,000. This payment must be made prior to the DSO recommending a student for OPT, pursuant to section 214.2(f)(11)(ii). SEVP may exercise discretion to grant a refund request from a school on a case-by-case basis, provided that the F-1 student has not been issued the OPT employment authorization document for the subsequent OPT, or granted OPT extension, associated with the fee that is subject to the refund request. This fee will not be refunded under any circumstances once the F-1 student is issued the employment authorization document for the OPT or OPT extension. The DSO must remove the student's OPT recommendation in SEVIS before requesting a refund. SEVP may issue further guidance on fee refund adjudications on the SEVP website at https://studyinthestates.dhs.gov (or successor uniform resource locator). Refund request determinations are not subject to any administrative appeals.
(3) 过渡期。对于在[最终规则公布之日后60天]当日或之后被DSO推荐参加任何类型OPT的每名外国人，学生及交流访问者计划（SEVP）认证的学校在推荐其参加OPT时必须支付70,000美元的费用。此后，对于此前已参加过OPT并已缴纳70,000美元OPT费用的任何F-1学生，DSO就其发出的任何后续OPT推荐，均须按每名外国人30,000美元的标准缴纳后续OPT费用。
(3) Transition period. SEVP-certified schools recommending any type of OPT for an alien must pay the fee of $70,000 for each alien who receives a DSO recommendation for OPT that is dated on or after [60 DAYS AFTER DATE OF PUBLICATION OF THE FINAL RULE]. Thereafter, any subsequent DSO recommendations for OPT issued for any F-1 student who has previously participated in OPT, and for which the $70,000 OPT fee has been paid, will be subject to the subsequent OPT fee of $30,000 per alien.
马克韦恩·穆林，
Markwayne Mullin,
美国国土安全部部长。
Secretary, U.S. Department of Homeland Security.

24. 自1947年以来，联邦法规一直规定，外国学生在其所就读教育机构要求或建议其从事实习培训工作并经移民官员批准的情况下可以工作。参见，例如，12 FR 5355, 5357（1947年8月7日）（“在学校要求或建议进行实习培训就业的情况下，地区主任可允许学生从事此类就业，为期六个月，并可延长不超过另外两个六个月的期限……”）；18 FR 3526, 3529（1953年6月19日）（“只要申请人所就读的机构或学习场所要求或建议进行实习培训就业，对该机构所在地具有行政管辖权的地区主任或主管官员可允许该外国人从事此类就业，为期六个月，并可延长不超过另外两个六个月的期限……”）；38 FR 35425, 35426（1973年12月28日）（“如果学生请求许可接受或继续从事就业以获得实习培训，获授权的学校官员必须证明该就业是为此目的而推荐的，并将为学生提供其所学专业领域的实习培训……”）。另见国会研究服务部（CRS），《美国外国学生选择性实习培训（OPT）》（2024年4月9日），可查阅网址：https://www.congress.gov/​crs-product/​IF12631。
24. Since 1947, federal regulations have provided that alien students may work in cases where employment for practical training is required or recommended by their educational institution and approved by immigration officials. See, e.g.,12 FR 5355, 5357 (Aug. 7, 1947) (“In cases where employment for practical training is required or recommended by the school, the district director may permit the student to engage in such employment for a six-month period subject to extension for not over two additional six-month periods . . .”); 18 FR 3526, 3529 (June 19, 1953) (“Whenever employment for practical training is required or recommended by the institution or place of study attended by the applicant, the district director or officer in charge having administrative jurisdiction over the place in which the institution is located may permit such employment of the alien for a six-month period subject to extension for not over two additional six-month periods . . .”); 38 FR 35425, 35426 (Dec. 28, 1973) (“If a student requests permission to accept or continue employment in order to obtain practical training, an authorized school official must certify that the employment is recommended for that purpose and will provide the student with practical training in his field of study . . .”). See also Congressional Research Service (CRS), Optional Practical Training (OPT) for Foreign Students in the United States (Apr. 9, 2024), available at https://www.congress.gov/​crs-product/​IF12631.

33. 参见《使H-1B要求现代化、为F-1项目提供灵活性并改进影响其他非移民工作者的项目》，89 FR 103054（2024年12月18日）。另见8 CFR 214.2(f)(5)(vi)。根据2008年和2016年的规则，配额间隙延期覆盖范围延长至10月1日。但在2024年，国土安全部修订了“配额间隙”期限，将覆盖范围进一步延长至申请H-1B身份所针对财政年度的4月1日，或所获批准申请的有效期开始日，以较早者为准。
33. See Modernizing H-1B Requirements, Providing Flexibility in the F-1 Program, and Program Improvements Affecting Other Nonimmigrant Workers,89 FR 103054 (Dec. 18, 2024). See also8 CFR 214.2(f)(5)(vi). Under the 2008 and 2016 rule, the cap-gap extension coverage extended until October 1. But in 2024, DHS amended the “cap-gap” period to further extend coverage until April 1 of the fiscal year for which the H-1B status is being requested or until the validity start date of the approved petition, whichever is earlier.

49. 就STEM OPT而言，STEM领域是指美国教育部《教学课程分类》分类法中属于工程学、生物科学、数学及物理科学二位数系列的领域，或相关领域。8 CFR 214.2(f)(10)(ii)(C)(2)。美国国土安全部(DHS)定期发布《DHS STEM指定学位课程清单》，其中列出DHS认定为符合24个月STEM OPT延期适用的STEM学习领域完整清单。参见美国移民及海关执法局，《DHS STEM指定学位课程清单》，可于以下网址查阅：https://www.ice.gov/​doclib/​sevis/​pdf/​stemList2024.pdf（最后更新日期：2024年7月22日）。
49. For purposes of STEM OPT, a STEM field is one included in the Department of Education's Classification of Instructional Programs taxonomy within the two-digit series containing engineering, biological sciences, mathematics, and physical sciences, or a related field. 8 CFR 214.2(f)(10)(ii)(C)(2). DHS periodically publishes the DHS STEM Designated Degree Program List which includes a complete list of fields of study that DHS considers to be STEM fields of study for purposes of the 24-month STEM OPT extension. See U.S. Immig. and Customs Enf't, DHS STEM Designated Degree Program List, available at https://www.ice.gov/​doclib/​sevis/​pdf/​stemList2024.pdf (last updated July 22, 2024).

51. 8 CFR 214.2(f)(11)(i)(C)。如果F-1非移民“及时且妥当地”提交I-765表，该学生的就业授权文件（EAD）将自动延期，直至USCIS审理完毕未决的I-765表，或延长至原EAD到期之日起最多180天，以两者中较早者为准。同上。另参见8 CFR 274a.12(b)(6)(iv)。
51. 8 CFR 214.2(f)(11)(i)(C). If the F-1 nonimmigrant “timely and properly” files Form I-765, the student's Employment Authorization Document (EAD) is automatically extended until USCIS adjudicates the pending Form I-765 or for up to 180 days from the date that the prior EAD expired, whichever comes earlier. Id. See also8 CFR 274a.12(b)(6)(iv).

52. 此类要求包括雇主参与E-Verify系统；院校、学生及雇主的报告义务，例如重大变更或提前终止/离职情况；以及学生就培训目标进展情况所提交的自我评估报告。8 CFR 214.2(f)(10)(ii)(C)(5)-(C)(10)；214.2(f)(11)，(f)(12)。

53. 8 CFR 214.2(f)(10)(ii)(C)(7)。在I-983表中，学生与雇主必须明确STEM OPT学习目标及培训计划，且雇主必须证明以下事项：其拥有足够资源提供此类培训；该学生不会取代美国工作者；培训条款及条件与处境相似的美国工作者相当；以及该STEM OPT将有助于该学生达成培训目标。参见8 CFR 214.2(f)(10)(ii)(C)(6)-(10)。
53. 8 CFR 214.2(f)(10)(ii)(C)( 7). On Form I-983, the student and employer must identify the STEM OPT learning objectives and a training plan, and the employer must attest the following: that it has sufficient resources to provide such training; that the student will not replace U.S. workers; that the terms and conditions of the training are commensurate with similarly situated U.S. workers; and that the STEM OPT will assist the student in reaching the training goals. See 8 CFR 214.2(f)(10)(ii)(C)( 6)-( 10).
返回引文
Back to Citation
54. 见8 CFR 214.2(f)(10)(ii)(C)(11)。在实地考察期间,国土安全部(DHS)核实F-1非移民学生在I-983表格中报告的有关培训机会的信息是否准确。实地考察仅限于核查与F-1非移民学生STEM OPT就业相关的信息,并确保F-1非移民学生和雇主所从事的实践学习经历与F-1非移民学生I-983表格中提供的信息一致。作为实地考察的一部分,国土安全部可以确认雇主是否拥有足够的资源和监督人员以有效维持该项目,并要求雇主提供其用于评估类似情况美国工人工资的证据。
54. See 8 CFR 214.2(f)(10)(ii)(C)( 11). During the site visit, DHS verifies that the information reported on the F-1 nonimmigrant student's Form I-983 about the training opportunity is accurate. Site visits are limited to checking information related to the F-1 nonimmigrant student's STEM OPT employment and ensuring that F-1 nonimmigrant students and employers are engaged in work-based learning experiences that are consistent with the information supplied on the F-1 nonimmigrant student's Form I-983. As part of a site visit, DHS may confirm that the employer has sufficient resources and supervisory personnel to effectively maintain the program and ask employers to provide the evidence they used to assess wages of similarly situated U.S. workers.

84. 一般参见第10973号总统公告,第90联邦公报46027页,《限制某些非移民工作者入境》(2025年9月24日)(“H-1B非移民签证项目的设立初衷是引进临时工作者来美从事附加性、高技能的工作,但该项目已被蓄意滥用,用薪资更低、技能更低的劳动力替代而非补充美国工人。通过系统性滥用该项目大规模替代美国工人的做法,已经损害了我国的经济安全与国家安全。一些雇主采用了现已被整个行业广泛采纳的做法,滥用H-1B法规及其条例人为压低工资,造成不利于美国公民的劳动力市场,同时也使吸引和留住技能最高的临时工作者群体变得更加困难,其中在关键的科学、技术、工程和数学(STEM)领域影响最大。”);第14150号行政命令,第90联邦公报8337页(2025年1月20日),《致国务卿的美国优先政策指令》(“美国的外交政策应当捍卫美国的核心利益,始终将美国和美国公民放在首位”);总统备忘录,《为美国家庭提供紧急价格缓解并击败生活成本危机》,第90联邦公报8245页(2025年1月20日)(指示各部门和机构“为美国工人创造就业机会,包括将灰心丧气的工人重新纳入劳动力队伍”)。
84. See generally, Proclamation No. 10973, 90 FR 46027, Restriction on Entry of Certain Nonimmigrant Workers (Sep. 24, 2025) (“The H-1B nonimmigrant visa program was created to bring temporary workers into the United States to perform additive, high-skilled functions, but it has been deliberately exploited to replace, rather than supplement, American workers with lower-paid, lower-skilled labor. The large-scale replacement of American workers through systemic abuse of the program has undermined both our economic and national security. Some employers, using practices now widely adopted by entire sectors, have abused the H-1B statute and its regulations to artificially suppress wages, resulting in a disadvantageous labor market for American citizens, while at the same time making it more difficult to attract and retain the highest skilled subset of temporary workers, with the largest impact seen in critical science, technology, engineering, and math (STEM) fields.”); Exec. Order No. 14150, 90 FR 8337 (Jan. 20, 2025), America First Policy Directive to the Secretary of State (“the foreign policy of the United States shall champion core American interests and always put America and American citizens first”); Presidential Memoranda, Delivering Emergency Price Relief for American Families and Defeating the Cost-of-Living Crisis,90 FR 8245 (Jan. 20, 2025) (directing departments and agencies to “create employment opportunities for American workers, including drawing discouraged workers into the labor force”).

102. 参见美国移民及海关执法局(ICE),《2024年SEVIS数据报告》(2025年6月5日),可查阅https://www.ice.gov/​doclib/​sevis/​btn/​25_​0605_​2024-sevis-btn.pdf。事实上,2008年,美国国土安全部(DHS)发布了STEM OPT临时最终规则,说明了H-1B签证可用数量的限制,并指出设立STEM OPT延期是为了解决美国高科技行业面临的直接竞争劣势以及学校吸引外国人才的能力问题。参见《联邦公报》第73卷第18944页,见前注30。然而,在2016年STEM OPT最终规则中,国土安全部重申,STEM OPT项目并非旨在规避H-1B签证项目的法定要求,包括国会设定的H-1B签证上限。参见《联邦公报》第81卷第13040页,见前注31。
102. See U.S. Immig. and Customs Enf't, 2024 SEVIS by the Numbers Report (June 5, 2025), available at https://www.ice.gov/​doclib/​sevis/​btn/​25_​0605_​2024-sevis-btn.pdf. Indeed, in 2008, DHS published the STEM OPT interim final rule, explaining the limitations on the number of H-1B visas available and stating that the STEM OPT extension was instituted to address the immediate competitive disadvantage faced by U.S. high-tech industries and the ability of schools to attract foreign talent. See 73 FR 18944, supra note 30. However, in the 2016 STEM OPT final rule, DHS reiterated that the STEM OPT program was not designed to circumvent the statutory requirements of the H-1B visa program, including the congressionally established cap on H-1B visas. See 81 FR 13040, supra note 31.

Back to Citation
103. 见前注76(“OPT未能包含许多本应由国会立法时纳入(或协商剔除)的常规保护措施,因为该项目是通过法规创设的,而非由国会制定,该法规在制定时并未分析安全管理一个最终将使H-1B项目相形见绌、并间接影响从事STEM或其他学科工作的美国工人的项目所需的资源。”)。
103. Supra note 76 (“OPT fails to include many of the customary protections that would have been legislated in (or negotiated out) had it been developed by Congress, rather than created by regulation that did not include analysis of necessary resources to securely administer a program that would eventually dwarf the H-1B program, and tangentially impact U.S. workers working in STEM or other disciplines.”).

106. See, e.g., Ctr. for Immig. Stud., ICE's Controversial Foreign Student Employment Programs Not Protecting U.S. Workers (Sept. 15, 2024), available at https://cis.org/​Report/​ICEs-Controversial-Foreign-Student-Employment-Programs-Not-Protecting-US-Workers (“[T]here is no penalty process built into the STEM OPT program as there is for employers who violate prevailing wage and related requirements of the H-1B program. . .”); Bloomberg, The STEM Graduate System Is Broken. Here's How to Fix It (Mar. 10, 2021), available at https://www.bloomberg.com/​graphics/​2021-opinion-optional-practical-training-problems-stem-graduates-deserve-better-jobs-opportunities/​ (“OPT has also opened a side door into the U.S. job market with minimal labor protections and oversight. It is increasingly funneling cheaper and more pliable, visa-dependent foreign candidates into fields such as software engineering and development, depressing wages and making a once-attractive career path less desirable. . . it can undercut American students looking for work.”); Economic Policy Institute, Congressional Testimony: The Impact of High-Skilled Immigration on U.S. Workers (Mar. 1, 2016), available at https://www.epi.org/​publication/​congressional-testimony-the-impact-of-high-skilled-immigration-on-u-s-workers-4/​ (“The L-1 visa and F-1 visa Optional Practical Training (OPT) programs . . . have no protections for American workers or foreign workers. There are no recruitment or non-displacement requirements for either program. American workers can and are replaced by these workers.”).

107. 经济政策研究所，《国会证词：高技能移民对美国工人的影响》（2016年3月1日），可在以下网址获取：https://www.epi.org/​publication/​congressional-testimony-the-impact-of-high-skilled-immigration-on-u-s-workers-4/​（“L-1签证及F-1签证的选择性实习训练（OPT）计划……对美国工人或外国工人均无保护。这两项计划均无招聘或非替代性要求。美国工人能够被、而且实际上正在被这些工人取代。”）（“STEM OPT计划通过豁免雇主缴纳联邦工资税的义务，使事实上的客籍劳工比美国工人显著更为廉价。这为雇主在STEM相关工作中雇用OPT雇员而非美国工人制造了经济激励，这对大多数可能是刚毕业、正在寻求入门级职位的STEM专业美国工人而言，是一个明显的不利因素。”）。
107. Economic Policy Institute, Congressional Testimony: The Impact of High-Skilled Immigration on U.S. Workers (Mar. 1, 2016), available at https://www.epi.org/​publication/​congressional-testimony-the-impact-of-high-skilled-immigration-on-u-s-workers-4/​ (“The L-1 visa and F-1 visa Optional Practical Training (OPT) programs . . . have no protections for American workers or foreign workers. There are no recruitment or non-displacement requirements for either program. American workers can and are replaced by these workers.”) (“The STEM OPT program makes de facto guestworkers significantly cheaper than U.S. workers by waiving the employer's obligation to pay federal payroll taxes. This creates a financial incentive for employers to hire OPT employees instead of U.S. workers in STEM jobs, which is an obvious disadvantage for U.S. workers, most of whom are likely to be recent STEM graduates seeking entry-level jobs.”).

108. 根据26 U.S.C. 3121(b)(19)规定，对于F-1非移民（以及其他若干非移民类别）在作为联邦税收意义上的非居民期间所提供的某些服务，可免征联邦保险缴款法（FICA）税。26 U.S.C. 3121(b)(19)。另见26 CFR 3121(b)(19)-1概述内容。STEM OPT参与者在开始其STEM OPT延期时，有可能已经累计满五个日历年，因而此后须缴纳FICA税。参见美国国土安全部（U.S. Dep't of Homeland Sec.），《留学美国》（Study in the States），《STEM OPT常见问题》，可在以下网址获取：https://studyinthestates.dhs.gov/​stem-opt-hub/​additional-resources/​stem-opt-frequently-asked-questions（最后访问日期：2025年9月9日）。尽管如此，F-1非移民及其雇主在其持F-1非移民身份的前五个日历年内，仍可能通过课程实习训练（CPT）及OPT就业享有FICA税豁免。因此，这仍可能构成雇主雇用F-1非移民而非美国工人的一项激励因素，这是一个真实且持续存在的担忧。
108. Under 26 U.S.C. 3121(b)(19), FICA taxation is exempted for certain services when they are performed by F-1 nonimmigrants (among other nonimmigrant classifications) who are nonresidents for Federal tax purposes for a period of time. 26 U.S.C. 3121(b)(19). See generally 26 CFR 3121(b)(19)-1. It is possible that STEM OPT participants will already have accrued five calendar years at the start of their STEM OPT extension and thus, be subject to FICA taxes thereafter. See U.S. Dep't of Homeland Sec ., Study in the States, STEM OPT Frequently Asked Questions, available at https://studyinthestates.dhs.gov/​stem-opt-hub/​additional-resources/​stem-opt-frequently-asked-questions (last visited Sept. 9, 2025). Nonetheless, the F-1 nonimmigrants and their employers potentially enjoy the FICA tax exemption through CPT and OPT employment during the first five calendar years in F-1 nonimmigrant status. As such, this could still be an incentive for employers to hire F-1 nonimmigrants over U.S. workers, which is a real and ongoing concern.

115. 美国国土安全部(DHS)认识到,8 CFR 214.1(c)条和214.2(f)(7)(v)条项下关于延长停留期(EOS)的申请要求,可能为DHS提供对F-1非移民学生进行额外审查的机会。参见《关于为非移民学术学生、交流访问者及外国信息媒体代表设定固定停留期限及延长停留程序的规定》,《联邦纪事》第91卷第44976页(2026年7月17日)。该最终规则(以下简称“停留期限最终规则”)将F类非移民的停留期限由“在身份期间内”改为固定时间段,并对入境及延期要求实施了其他修改。然而,并非所有F-1非移民学生都须申请延长停留期以从事选择性实习训练(OPT),例如从事修业完成前OPT的学生,或根据“停留期限最终规则”享有既往利益保护并受8 CFR 214.2(m)条规定约束的学生。DHS认为,现行有效的法规与拟议的OPT收费规则相结合,将为DHS有效监管OPT项目提供全面且整体的方法。
115. DHS recognizes that the application requirements for EOS under 8 CFR 214.1(c) and 214.2(f)(7)(v) may provide DHS with additional opportunities to vet F-1 nonimmigrant students. See “Establishing a Fixed Time Period of Admission and an Extension of Stay Procedure for Nonimmigrant Academic Students, Exchange Visitors, and Representatives of Foreign Information Media,” 91 FR 44976 (July 17, 2026). This final rule (hereinafter “Duration of Status Final Rule”) amended the admission period for F nonimmigrant classification from duration of status to a fixed time period and implemented additional changes to admission and extension requirements. However, not all F-1 nonimmigrant students are required to apply for an EOS to engage OPT, such as those participating in pre-completion OPT or those grandfathered under the Duration of Status Final Rule and subject to the regulations at 8 CFR 214.2(m). DHS believes that the regulations currently in effect, together with the proposed OPT fee rule, will provide a comprehensive and holistic approach for DHS to effectively regulate the OPT program.

130. 转移支付是指在不影响社会可用资源总量的情况下,一方向另一方支付的款项。拟议收费将在私营实体与政府之间转移资源,而不一定改变社会资源总量。参见OMB通告A-4号,《监管分析》第38页(2003年9月17日),可查阅网址:https://www.whitehouse.gov/​wp-content/​uploads/​2025/​08/​CircularA-4.pdf。可查阅网址:https://www.whitehouse.gov/​wp-content/​uploads/​2025/​08/​CircularA-4.pdf。
